* Effect Service Template\\\\n * \\\\n * This template demonstrates the correct patterns for creating Effect services\\\\n * based on issues identified in docs/fixes/\\\\n * \\\\n * Copy this file and replace \\\\\\\"Example\\\\\\\" with your service name.\\\\n */\\\\n\\\\nimport { Context, Effect, Layer, pipe } from 'effect'\\\\nimport type { ConfigError } from 'effect/ConfigError'\\\\nimport { type UnknownException } from 'effect/Cause'\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Step 1: Define your service errors\\\\n * Always create specific error types for your service\\\\n */\\\\nexport class ExampleServiceError extends Error {\\\\n  readonly _tag = 'ExampleServiceError'\\\\n  \\\\n  constructor(\\\\n    message: string,\\\\n    readonly cause?: unknown\\\\n  ) {\\\\n    super(message)\\\\n    this.name = 'ExampleServiceError'\\\\n  }\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Step 2: Define your service interface\\\\n * This is what consumers will interact with\\\\n */\\\\nexport interface ExampleService {\\\\n  readonly doSomething: (input: string) => Effect.Effect<string, ExampleServiceError>\\\\n  readonly doSomethingAsync: (input: number) => Effect.Effect<number, ExampleServiceError>\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Step 3: Create the service Tag\\\\n * IMPORTANT: Use Context.GenericTag, not Context.Tag (fixes type inference issues)\\\\n */\\\\nexport const ExampleService = Context.GenericTag<ExampleService>('ExampleService')\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Step 4: Define configuration interface if needed\\\\n */\\\\nexport interface ExampleServiceConfig {\\\\n  readonly apiUrl: string\\\\n  readonly timeout: number\\\\n  readonly enableDebug?: boolean\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Step 5: Create configuration service tag\\\\n */\\\\nexport const ExampleServiceConfig = Context.GenericTag<ExampleServiceConfig>('ExampleServiceConfig')\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Step 6: Implement the service\\\\n * Use a class for complex services, object literal for simple ones\\\\n */\\\\nexport class ExampleServiceImpl implements ExampleService {\\\\n  constructor(\\\\n    private readonly config: ExampleServiceConfig\\\\n  ) {}\\\\n\\\\n  readonly doSomething = (input: string) =>\\\\n    Effect.gen(function* () {\\\\n      try {\\\\n        // Your implementation here\\\\n        if (input.length === 0) {\\\\n          return yield* Effect.fail(\\\\n            new ExampleServiceError('Input cannot be empty')\\\\n          )\\\\n        }\\\\n        \\\\n        if (this.config.enableDebug) {\\\\n          yield* Effect.log(`Processing: ${input}`)\\\\n        }\\\\n        \\\\n        return `Processed: ${input}`\\\\n      } catch (error) {\\\\n        return yield* Effect.fail(\\\\n          new ExampleServiceError('Failed to process input', error)\\\\n        )\\\\n      }\\\\n    })\\\\n\\\\n  readonly doSomethingAsync = (input: number) =>\\\\n    Effect.gen(function* () {\\\\n      try {\\\\n        // Simulate async operation\\\\n        yield* Effect.sleep(100)\\\\n        \\\\n        if (input < 0) {\\\\n          return yield* Effect.fail(\\\\n            new ExampleServiceError('Input must be positive')\\\\n          )\\\\n        }\\\\n        \\\\n        // Example of calling external API with timeout\\\\n        const result = yield* pipe(\\\\n          Effect.promise(() => \\\\n            fetch(`${this.config.apiUrl}/data/${input}`)\\\\n              .then(res => res.json())\\\\n              .then(data => data.value as number)\\\\n          ),\\\\n          Effect.timeout(this.config.timeout),\\\\n          Effect.catchAll((error) => \\\\n            Effect.fail(new ExampleServiceError('API call failed', error))\\\\n          )\\\\n        )\\\\n        \\\\n        return result\\\\n      } catch (error) {\\\\n        return yield* Effect.fail(\\\\n          new ExampleServiceError('Async operation failed', error)\\\\n        )\\\\n      }\\\\n    })\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Step 7: Create the service Layer\\\\n * This is how the service is provided to the application\\\\n */\\\\nexport const ExampleServiceLive = Layer.effect(\\\\n  ExampleService,\\\\n  Effect.gen(function* () {\\\\n    const config = yield* ExampleServiceConfig\\\\n    return new ExampleServiceImpl(config)\\\\n  })\\\\n)\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Step 8: Create a test/mock implementation\\\\n */\\\\nexport const ExampleServiceTest = Layer.succeed(\\\\n  ExampleService,\\\\n  {\\\\n    doSomething: (input: string) => Effect.succeed(`Test: ${input}`),\\\\n    doSomethingAsync: (input: number) => Effect.succeed(input * 2)\\\\n  }\\\\n)\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Step 9: Create configuration layers\\\\n */\\\\nexport const ExampleServiceConfigLive = (config: ExampleServiceConfig) =>\\\\n  Layer.succeed(ExampleServiceConfig, config)\\\\n\\\\nexport const ExampleServiceConfigDefault = Layer.succeed(\\\\n  ExampleServiceConfig,\\\\n  {\\\\n    apiUrl: 'https://api.example.com',\\\\n    timeout: 5000,\\\\n    enableDebug: false\\\\n  }\\\\n)\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Step 10: Compose layers for easy consumption\\\\n */\\\\nexport const ExampleServiceDefault = ExampleServiceConfigDefault.pipe(\\\\n  Layer.provide(ExampleServiceLive)\\\\n)\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Step 11: Export type helpers\\\\n */\\\\nexport type ExampleServiceShape = Context.Tag.Service<typeof ExampleService>\\\"}\"}},{\"id\":\"toolu_01KZDWfKoHpdunkUmjUr4NaA\",\"type\":\"function\",\"function\":{\"name\":\"Write\",\"arguments\":\"{\\\"file_path\\\":\\\"/Users/christopherdavid/code/commander/src/services/patterns/EffectReactHooks.ts\\\",\\\"content\\\":\\\"/**\\\\n * Effect React Hooks\\\\n * \\\\n * Safe patterns for using Effect services in React components\\\\n * Addresses issues from docs/fixes/023-effect-runtime-stale-references.md\\\\n */\\\\n\\\\nimport { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'\\\\nimport { Effect, Exit, Runtime, pipe } from 'effect'\\\\nimport type { Context } from 'effect'\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Hook for using Effect services in React components\\\\n * Prevents stale reference issues by properly managing the runtime lifecycle\\\\n */\\\\nexport function useEffectService<A, E, R>(\\\\n  effect: Effect.Effect<A, E, R>,\\\\n  runtime: Runtime.Runtime<R>,\\\\n  deps: React.DependencyList = []\\\\n): {\\\\n  data: A | null\\\\n  error: E | null\\\\n  loading: boolean\\\\n  execute: () => void\\\\n} {\\\\n  const [data, setData] = useState<A | null>(null)\\\\n  const [error, setError] = useState<E | null>(null)\\\\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)\\\\n  \\\\n  // Use ref to store the latest runtime to prevent stale closures\\\\n  const runtimeRef = useRef(runtime)\\\\n  useEffect(() => {\\\\n    runtimeRef.current = runtime\\\\n  }, [runtime])\\\\n  \\\\n  // Store abort controller to cancel in-flight requests\\\\n  const abortControllerRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null)\\\\n  \\\\n  const execute = useCallback(() => {\\\\n    // Cancel any in-flight request\\\\n    if (abortControllerRef.current) {\\\\n      abortControllerRef.current.abort()\\\\n    }\\\\n    \\\\n    const abortController = new AbortController()\\\\n    abortControllerRef.current = abortController\\\\n    \\\\n    setLoading(true)\\\\n    setError(null)\\\\n    \\\\n    const fiber = Effect.runFork(effect, {\\\\n      signal: abortController.signal\\\\n    })(runtimeRef.current)\\\\n    \\\\n    fiber.await.then((exit) => {\\\\n      if (!abortController.signal.aborted) {\\\\n        if (Exit.isSuccess(exit)) {\\\\n          setData(exit.value)\\\\n          setError(null)\\\\n        } else {\\\\n          setData(null)\\\\n          setError(exit.cause as unknown as E)\\\\n        }\\\\n        setLoading(false)\\\\n      }\\\\n    })\\\\n    \\\\n    return () => {\\\\n      abortController.abort()\\\\n    }\\\\n  }, [...deps, effect])\\\\n  \\\\n  // Cleanup on unmount\\\\n  useEffect(() => {\\\\n    return () => {\\\\n      if (abortControllerRef.current) {\\\\n        abortControllerRef.current.abort()\\\\n      }\\\\n    }\\\\n  }, [])\\\\n  \\\\n  return { data, error, loading, execute }\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Hook for using Effect services with automatic execution\\\\n */\\\\nexport function useEffectServiceAuto<A, E, R>(\\\\n  effect: Effect.Effect<A, E, R>,\\\\n  runtime: Runtime.Runtime<R>,\\\\n  deps: React.DependencyList = []\\\\n) {\\\\n  const result = useEffectService(effect, runtime, deps)\\\\n  \\\\n  useEffect(() => {\\\\n    result.execute()\\\\n  }, [result.execute])\\\\n  \\\\n  return result\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Hook for creating a stable Effect runtime in React\\\\n * Prevents recreation on every render\\\\n */\\\\nexport function useEffectRuntime<R>(\\\\n  layer: Effect.Layer<R, never, never>,\\\\n  deps: React.DependencyList = []\\\\n): Runtime.Runtime<R> | null {\\\\n  const [runtime, setRuntime] = useState<Runtime.Runtime<R> | null>(null)\\\\n  \\\\n  useEffect(() => {\\\\n    let canceled = false\\\\n    \\\\n    Effect.runPromise(\\\\n      pipe(\\\\n        Effect.runtime<R>(),\\\\n        Effect.provide(layer)\\\\n      )\\\\n    ).then((rt) => {\\\\n      if (!canceled) {\\\\n        setRuntime(rt)\\\\n      }\\\\n    })\\\\n    \\\\n    return () => {\\\\n      canceled = true\\\\n    }\\\\n  }, deps)\\\\n  \\\\n  return runtime\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Hook for accessing a specific service from the runtime\\\\n */\\\\nexport function useService<T>(\\\\n  tag: Context.Tag<T, T>,\\\\n  runtime: Runtime.Runtime<any> | null\\\\n): T | null {\\\\n  const [service, setService] = useState<T | null>(null)\\\\n  \\\\n  useEffect(() => {\\\\n    if (runtime) {\\\\n      const context = runtime.context\\\\n      if (Context.isContext(context)) {\\\\n        const serviceInstance = Context.getOption(context, tag)\\\\n        if (serviceInstance._tag === 'Some') {\\\\n          setService(serviceInstance.value)\\\\n        }\\\\n      }\\\\n    }\\\\n  }, [runtime, tag])\\\\n  \\\\n  return service\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Hook for streaming Effect values\\\\n * Useful for real-time updates, progress tracking, etc.\\\\n */\\\\nexport function useEffectStream<A, E, R>(\\\\n  stream: Effect.Stream<A, E, R>,\\\\n  runtime: Runtime.Runtime<R>,\\\\n  onValue: (value: A) => void,\\\\n  onError?: (error: E) => void,\\\\n  deps: React.DependencyList = []\\\\n) {\\\\n  const runtimeRef = useRef(runtime)\\\\n  useEffect(() => {\\\\n    runtimeRef.current = runtime\\\\n  }, [runtime])\\\\n  \\\\n  useEffect(() => {\\\\n    const fiber = Effect.runFork(\\\\n      stream.pipe(\\\\n        Effect.tap((value) => Effect.sync(() => onValue(value))),\\\\n        Effect.catchAll((error) =>\\\\n          Effect.sync(() => {\\\\n            if (onError) onError(error)\\\\n          })\\\\n        )\\\\n      )\\\\n    )(runtimeRef.current)\\\\n    \\\\n    return () => {\\\\n      fiber.interrupt\\\\n    }\\\\n  }, deps)\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Error boundary component for Effect services\\\\n */\\\\nexport class EffectErrorBoundary extends React.Component<\\\\n  {\\\\n    children: React.ReactNode\\\\n    fallback: (error: unknown) => React.ReactNode\\\\n  },\\\\n  { hasError: boolean; error: unknown }\\\\n> {\\\\n  constructor(props: any) {\\\\n    super(props)\\\\n    this.state = { hasError: false, error: null }\\\\n  }\\\\n  \\\\n  static getDerivedStateFromError(error: unknown) {\\\\n    return { hasError: true, error }\\\\n  }\\\\n  \\\\n  componentDidCatch(error: Error, errorInfo: React.ErrorInfo) {\\\\n    console.error('Effect service error:', error, errorInfo)\\\\n  }\\\\n  \\\\n  render() {\\\\n    if (this.state.hasError) {\\\\n      return this.props.fallback(this.state.error)\\\\n    }\\\\n    \\\\n    return this.props.children\\\\n  }\\\\n}\\\"}\"}},{\"id\":\"toolu_0175Q5ugorSTdZsn7VptP19j\",\"type\":\"function\",\"function\":{\"name\":\"Write\",\"arguments\":\"{\\\"file_path\\\":\\\"/Users/christopherdavid/code/commander/src/services/patterns/TestServiceMocks.ts\\\",\\\"content\\\":\\\"/**\\\\n * Test Service Mocks Template\\\\n * \\\\n * Patterns for creating test mocks that satisfy Effect's type system\\\\n * Addresses issues from docs/fixes/012-strategic-test-type-casting.md\\\\n */\\\\n\\\\nimport { Context, Effect, Layer, pipe } from 'effect'\\\\nimport type { ConfigError } from 'effect/ConfigError'\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Pattern 1: Simple mock with static values\\\\n */\\\\nexport const createSimpleMock = <T>(\\\\n  tag: Context.Tag<T, T>,\\\\n  implementation: T\\\\n): Layer.Layer<T> => {\\\\n  return Layer.succeed(tag, implementation)\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Pattern 2: Mock with configurable behavior\\\\n */\\\\nexport interface MockConfig<T> {\\\\n  implementation: Partial<T>\\\\n  delays?: Record<string, number>\\\\n  errors?: Record<string, Error>\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\nexport const createConfigurableMock = <T extends Record<string, any>>(\\\\n  tag: Context.Tag<T, T>,\\\\n  config: MockConfig<T>\\\\n): Layer.Layer<T> => {\\\\n  const implementation = {} as T\\\\n  \\\\n  for (const key in config.implementation) {\\\\n    const method = config.implementation[key]\\\\n    if (typeof method === 'function') {\\\\n      implementation[key] = ((...args: any[]) => {\\\\n        // Add configured delay\\\\n        const delay = config.delays?.[key] || 0\\\\n        \\\\n        // Check for configured error\\\\n        const error = config.errors?.[key]\\\\n        \\\\n        return pipe(\\\\n          Effect.sleep(delay),\\\\n          Effect.flatMap(() => \\\\n            error \\\\n              ? Effect.fail(error)\\\\n              : Effect.succeed(method(...args))\\\\n          )\\\\n        )\\\\n      }) as any\\\\n    } else {\\\\n      implementation[key] = method as any\\\\n    }\\\\n  }\\\\n  \\\\n  return Layer.succeed(tag, implementation)\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Pattern 3: Spy mock that records calls\\\\n */\\\\nexport interface SpyMock<T> {\\\\n  implementation: T\\\\n  calls: Record<string, any[][]>\\\\n  resetCalls: () => void\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\nexport const createSpyMock = <T extends Record<string, any>>(\\\\n  tag: Context.Tag<T, T>,\\\\n  baseImplementation: T\\\\n): Layer.Layer<T> & { spy: SpyMock<T> } => {\\\\n  const calls: Record<string, any[][]> = {}\\\\n  \\\\n  const implementation = {} as T\\\\n  \\\\n  for (const key in baseImplementation) {\\\\n    const method = baseImplementation[key]\\\\n    if (typeof method === 'function') {\\\\n      calls[key] = []\\\\n      implementation[key] = ((...args: any[]) => {\\\\n        calls[key].push(args)\\\\n        return method(...args)\\\\n      }) as any\\\\n    } else {\\\\n      implementation[key] = method\\\\n    }\\\\n  }\\\\n  \\\\n  const spy: SpyMock<T> = {\\\\n    implementation,\\\\n    calls,\\\\n    resetCalls: () => {\\\\n      for (const key in calls) {\\\\n        calls[key] = []\\\\n      }\\\\n    }\\\\n  }\\\\n  \\\\n  const layer = Layer.succeed(tag, implementation) as Layer.Layer<T> & { spy: SpyMock<T> }\\\\n  layer.spy = spy\\\\n  \\\\n  return layer\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Pattern 4: State-based mock\\\\n */\\\\nexport interface StatefulMock<T, S> {\\\\n  implementation: T\\\\n  getState: () => S\\\\n  setState: (state: S) => void\\\\n  resetState: () => void\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\nexport const createStatefulMock = <T extends Record<string, any>, S>(\\\\n  tag: Context.Tag<T, T>,\\\\n  initialState: S,\\\\n  implementationFactory: (\\\\n    getState: () => S,\\\\n    setState: (state: S) => void\\\\n  ) => T\\\\n): Layer.Layer<T> & { mock: StatefulMock<T, S> } => {\\\\n  let state = initialState\\\\n  \\\\n  const getState = () => state\\\\n  const setState = (newState: S) => { state = newState }\\\\n  const resetState = () => { state = initialState }\\\\n  \\\\n  const implementation = implementationFactory(getState, setState)\\\\n  \\\\n  const mock: StatefulMock<T, S> = {\\\\n    implementation,\\\\n    getState,\\\\n    setState,\\\\n    resetState\\\\n  }\\\\n  \\\\n  const layer = Layer.succeed(tag, implementation) as Layer.Layer<T> & { mock: StatefulMock<T, S> }\\\\n  layer.mock = mock\\\\n  \\\\n  return layer\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Pattern 5: Async mock with promise resolution control\\\\n */\\\\nexport interface AsyncMock<T> {\\\\n  implementation: T\\\\n  resolvers: Record<string, {\\\\n    resolve: (value: any) => void\\\\n    reject: (error: any) => void\\\\n  }[]>\\\\n  waitForCall: (method: string, callIndex?: number) => Promise<void>\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\nexport const createAsyncMock = <T extends Record<string, any>>(\\\\n  tag: Context.Tag<T, T>,\\\\n  methods: (keyof T)[]\\\\n): Layer.Layer<T> & { async: AsyncMock<T> } => {\\\\n  const resolvers: Record<string, any[]> = {}\\\\n  const pendingCalls: Record<string, Promise<void>[]> = {}\\\\n  \\\\n  const implementation = {} as T\\\\n  \\\\n  for (const method of methods) {\\\\n    resolvers[method as string] = []\\\\n    pendingCalls[method as string] = []\\\\n    \\\\n    implementation[method as string] = (() => {\\\\n      return Effect.async((resume) => {\\\\n        const promise = new Promise<void>((resolve) => {\\\\n          const resolverObj = {\\\\n            resolve: (value: any) => {\\\\n              resume(Effect.succeed(value))\\\\n              resolve()\\\\n            },\\\\n            reject: (error: any) => {\\\\n              resume(Effect.fail(error))\\\\n              resolve()\\\\n            }\\\\n          }\\\\n          resolvers[method as string].push(resolverObj)\\\\n        })\\\\n        pendingCalls[method as string].push(promise)\\\\n      })\\\\n    }) as any\\\\n  }\\\\n  \\\\n  const asyncMock: AsyncMock<T> = {\\\\n    implementation,\\\\n    resolvers: resolvers as any,\\\\n    waitForCall: async (method: string, callIndex = 0) => {\\\\n      const calls = pendingCalls[method]\\\\n      if (calls && calls[callIndex]) {\\\\n        await calls[callIndex]\\\\n      }\\\\n    }\\\\n  }\\\\n  \\\\n  const layer = Layer.succeed(tag, implementation) as Layer.Layer<T> & { async: AsyncMock<T> }\\\\n  layer.async = asyncMock\\\\n  \\\\n  return layer\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Example usage with a hypothetical UserService\\\\n */\\\\nexport interface UserService {\\\\n  getUser: (id: string) => Effect.Effect<{ id: string; name: string }, Error>\\\\n  updateUser: (id: string, name: string) => Effect.Effect<void, Error>\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\nexport const UserService = Context.GenericTag<UserService>('UserService')\\\\n\\\\n// Example 1: Simple mock\\\\nexport const UserServiceSimpleMock = createSimpleMock(UserService, {\\\\n  getUser: (id: string) => Effect.succeed({ id, name: 'Test User' }),\\\\n  updateUser: () => Effect.succeed(undefined)\\\\n})\\\\n\\\\n// Example 2: Spy mock\\\\nexport const UserServiceSpyMock = createSpyMock(UserService, {\\\\n  getUser: (id: string) => Effect.succeed({ id, name: 'Test User' }),\\\\n  updateUser: () => Effect.succeed(undefined)\\\\n})\\\\n\\\\n// Example 3: Stateful mock\\\\nexport const UserServiceStatefulMock = createStatefulMock(\\\\n  UserService,\\\\n  { users: new Map<string, string>() },\\\\n  (getState, setState) => ({\\\\n    getUser: (id: string) =>\\\\n      Effect.gen(function* () {\\\\n        const state = getState()\\\\n        const name = state.users.get(id)\\\\n        if (!name) {\\\\n          return yield* Effect.fail(new Error('User not found'))\\\\n        }\\\\n        return { id, name }\\\\n      }),\\\\n    updateUser: (id: string, name: string) =>\\\\n      Effect.sync(() => {\\\\n        const state = getState()\\\\n        state.users.set(id, name)\\\\n        setState(state)\\\\n      })\\\\n  })\\\\n)\\\\n\\\\n// Test helpers\\\\nexport const runEffectTest = async <A, E>(\\\\n  effect: Effect.Effect<A, E, any>,\\\\n  layer: Layer.Layer<any>\\\\n): Promise<A> => {\\\\n  return Effect.runPromise(pipe(effect, Effect.provide(layer)))\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\nexport const runEffectTestExpectError = async <A, E>(\\\\n  effect: Effect.Effect<A, E, any>,\\\\n  layer: Layer.Layer<any>\\\\n): Promise<E> => {\\\\n  try {\\\\n    await Effect.runPromise(pipe(effect, Effect.provide(layer)))\\\\n    throw new Error('Expected effect to fail')\\\\n  } catch (error) {\\\\n    return error as E\\\\n  }\\\\n}\\\"}\"}},{\"id\":\"toolu_01GY7PDGqioqpmFZAhLTSmzd\",\"type\":\"function\",\"function\":{\"name\":\"Write\",\"arguments\":\"{\\\"file_path\\\":\\\"/Users/christopherdavid/code/commander/src/services/patterns/EffectCheatSheet.md\\\",\\\"content\\\":\\\"# Effect-TS Cheat Sheet for OpenAgents Commander\\\\n\\\\nThis cheat sheet covers common Effect patterns and solutions to issues found in docs/fixes/.\\\\n\\\\n## Table of Contents\\\\n1. [Service Creation Patterns](#service-creation-patterns)\\\\n2. [Error Handling](#error-handling)  \\\\n3. [Configuration Management](#configuration-management)\\\\n4. [Testing Patterns](#testing-patterns)\\\\n5. [React Integration](#react-integration)\\\\n6. [Common Gotchas](#common-gotchas)\\\\n\\\\n## Service Creation Patterns\\\\n\\\\n### ✅ DO: Use Context.GenericTag\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// Correct - provides better type inference\\\\nexport const MyService = Context.GenericTag<MyService>('MyService')\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### ❌ DON'T: Use Context.Tag\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// Incorrect - can cause type inference issues\\\\nexport const MyService = Context.Tag<MyService>('MyService')\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### ✅ DO: Create specific error types\\\\n```typescript\\\\nexport class MyServiceError extends Error {\\\\n  readonly _tag = 'MyServiceError'\\\\n  constructor(message: string, readonly cause?: unknown) {\\\\n    super(message)\\\\n  }\\\\n}\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### ✅ DO: Use Layer.effect for complex services\\\\n```typescript\\\\nexport const MyServiceLive = Layer.effect(\\\\n  MyService,\\\\n  Effect.gen(function* () {\\\\n    const config = yield* ConfigService\\\\n    const database = yield* DatabaseService\\\\n    return new MyServiceImpl(config, database)\\\\n  })\\\\n)\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n## Error Handling\\\\n\\\\n### Handle different error types\\\\n```typescript\\\\nEffect.catchTags({\\\\n  ConfigError: (error) => Effect.fail(new MyServiceError('Invalid config', error)),\\\\n  NetworkError: (error) => Effect.fail(new MyServiceError('Network failed', error)),\\\\n  // Catch-all for unexpected errors\\\\n  UnknownException: (error) => Effect.fail(new MyServiceError('Unknown error', error))\\\\n})\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### Convert Promise errors safely\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// ✅ DO: Wrap promises properly\\\\nEffect.tryPromise({\\\\n  try: () => fetch(url),\\\\n  catch: (error) => new MyServiceError('Fetch failed', error)\\\\n})\\\\n\\\\n// ❌ DON'T: Use Effect.promise directly\\\\nEffect.promise(() => fetch(url)) // Loses error context\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n## Configuration Management\\\\n\\\\n### ✅ DO: Forbid fallbacks for sensitive data (Fix #022)\\\\n```typescript\\\\nexport const ApiKeyConfig = Config.string('API_KEY').pipe(\\\\n  Config.validate({\\\\n    message: 'API_KEY is required',\\\\n    validation: (key) => key.length > 0\\\\n  })\\\\n)\\\\n\\\\n// ❌ DON'T: Use withDefault for secrets\\\\nConfig.string('API_KEY').pipe(Config.withDefault('test-key'))\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### ✅ DO: Create typed configuration services\\\\n```typescript\\\\nexport interface MyServiceConfig {\\\\n  readonly apiUrl: string\\\\n  readonly timeout: number\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\nexport const MyServiceConfig = Context.GenericTag<MyServiceConfig>('MyServiceConfig')\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n## Testing Patterns\\\\n\\\\n### Use test layers properly\\\\n```typescript\\\\ndescribe('MyService', () => {\\\\n  const testLayer = Layer.mergeAll(\\\\n    DatabaseServiceTest,\\\\n    ConfigServiceTest,\\\\n    MyServiceLive\\\\n  )\\\\n  \\\\n  it('should work', async () => {\\\\n    const result = await Effect.runPromise(\\\\n      myServiceMethod.pipe(Effect.provide(testLayer))\\\\n    )\\\\n    expect(result).toBe(expected)\\\\n  })\\\\n})\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### Mock with spy capabilities\\\\n```typescript\\\\nconst mockLayer = createSpyMock(MyService, {\\\\n  doSomething: () => Effect.succeed('mocked')\\\\n})\\\\n\\\\n// After test\\\\nexpect(mockLayer.spy.calls.doSomething).toHaveLength(1)\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n## React Integration\\\\n\\\\n### ✅ DO: Use refs to prevent stale closures (Fix #023)\\\\n```typescript\\\\nconst runtimeRef = useRef(runtime)\\\\nuseEffect(() => {\\\\n  runtimeRef.current = runtime\\\\n}, [runtime])\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### ✅ DO: Cleanup Effect fibers\\\\n```typescript\\\\nuseEffect(() => {\\\\n  const fiber = Effect.runFork(myEffect)(runtime)\\\\n  return () => {\\\\n    fiber.interrupt\\\\n  }\\\\n}, [deps])\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### ❌ DON'T: Create runtime in render\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// Bad - creates new runtime every render\\\\nconst runtime = Runtime.make(layer)\\\\n\\\\n// Good - use useEffectRuntime hook\\\\nconst runtime = useEffectRuntime(layer)\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n## Common Gotchas\\\\n\\\\n### 1. Double-yield in Layer.effect (Fix #014)\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// ❌ Wrong - double yield\\\\nLayer.effect(\\\\n  MyService,\\\\n  Effect.gen(function* () {\\\\n    return yield* Effect.succeed(new MyServiceImpl())\\\\n  })\\\\n)\\\\n\\\\n// ✅ Correct - single yield or direct return\\\\nLayer.effect(\\\\n  MyService,\\\\n  Effect.gen(function* () {\\\\n    const config = yield* ConfigService\\\\n    return new MyServiceImpl(config)\\\\n  })\\\\n)\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### 2. Service dependency cycles\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// ❌ Circular dependency\\\\nconst ServiceA = Layer.effect(ServiceATag, \\\\n  Effect.gen(function* () {\\\\n    const b = yield* ServiceB // ServiceB depends on ServiceA!\\\\n  })\\\\n)\\\\n\\\\n// ✅ Use factory pattern or restructure\\\\nconst ServiceAFactory = (serviceB: ServiceB) => \\\\n  Layer.succeed(ServiceATag, new ServiceAImpl(serviceB))\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### 3. Effect vs Promise in APIs\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// When exposing to non-Effect code\\\\nexport class MyServiceBridge {\\\\n  async doSomething(input: string): Promise<Result> {\\\\n    return Effect.runPromise(\\\\n      this.service.doSomething(input).pipe(\\\\n        Effect.provide(this.runtime)\\\\n      )\\\\n    )\\\\n  }\\\\n}\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### 4. Type inference with pipe\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// Help TypeScript with explicit types when needed\\\\npipe(\\\\n  myEffect,\\\\n  Effect.map((value): ProcessedType => processValue(value)),\\\\n  Effect.catchAll((error): Effect.Effect<Fallback, never> => \\\\n    Effect.succeed(fallbackValue)\\\\n  )\\\\n)\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### 5. Testing async Effects\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// Use Effect.runPromise in tests\\\\nit('should handle async operations', async () => {\\\\n  const result = await Effect.runPromise(\\\\n    myAsyncEffect.pipe(Effect.provide(testLayer))\\\\n  )\\\\n  expect(result).toBe(expected)\\\\n})\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n## Quick Reference\\\\n\\\\n### Essential imports\\\\n```typescript\\\\nimport { Context, Effect, Layer, pipe, Stream } from 'effect'\\\\nimport * as Config from 'effect/Config'\\\\nimport * as Runtime from 'effect/Runtime'\\\\nimport * as Exit from 'effect/Exit'\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### Service lifecycle\\\\n1. Define interface → 2. Create Tag → 3. Implement → 4. Create Layer → 5. Compose\\\\n\\\\n### Effect operators by use case\\\\n- **Transform**: `map`, `flatMap`, `tap`\\\\n- **Error handling**: `catchAll`, `catchTags`, `orElse`\\\\n- **Resource management**: `acquireRelease`, `scoped`\\\\n- **Concurrency**: `all`, `race`, `forEach`\\\\n- **Retry**: `retry`, `retryOrElse`\\\\n- **Timeout**: `timeout`, `timeoutFail`\\\\n\\\\n### Debug helpers\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// Log intermediate values\\\\nEffect.tap(value => Effect.log('Value:', value))\\\\n\\\\n// Log and pass through\\\\nEffect.tapBoth({\\\\n  onFailure: (error) => Effect.log('Error:', error),\\\\n  onSuccess: (value) => Effect.log('Success:', value)\\\\n})\\\\n```\\\"}\"}}]","timestamp":1748307511}}

React.DependencyList = []\\\\n) {\\\\n  const result = useEffectService(effect, runtime, deps)\\\\n  \\\\n  useEffect(() => {\\\\n    result.execute()\\\\n  }, [result.execute])\\\\n  \\\\n  return result\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Hook for creating a stable Effect runtime in React\\\\n * Prevents recreation on every render\\\\n */\\\\nexport function useEffectRuntime<R>(\\\\n  layer: Effect.Layer<R, never, never>,\\\\n  deps: React.DependencyList = []\\\\n): Runtime.Runtime<R> | null {\\\\n  const [runtime, setRuntime] = useState<Runtime.Runtime<R> | null>(null)\\\\n  \\\\n runtime\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Hook for accessing a specific service from the runtime\\\\n */\\\\nexport function useService<T>(\\\\n  tag: Context.Tag<T, T>,\\\\n  runtime: Runtime.Runtime<any> | (Context.isContext(context)) {\\\\n        const serviceInstance = Context.getOption(context, tag)\\\\n        if (serviceInstance._tag === 'Some') {\\\\n return () => {\\\\n      fiber.interrupt\\\\n    }\\\\n  }, deps)\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Error boundary component for Effect services\\\\n */\\\\nexport class EffectErrorBoundary extends React.Component<\\\\n  {\\\\n    children: React.ReactNode\\\\n    fallback: (error: unknown) => React.ReactNode\\\\n  },\\\\n  { hasError: boolean; error: unknown }\\\\n> {\\\\n  constructor(props: any) {\\\\n    super(props)\\\\n    this.state = { hasError: false, error: null }\\\\n  }\\\\n  \\\\n  static getDerivedStateFromError(error: unknown) {\\\\n    return { hasError: true, error }\\\\n * Test Service Mocks Template\\\\n * \\\\n * Patterns for creating test mocks that satisfy Effect's type system\\\\n * Addresses issues from docs/fixes/012-strategic-test-type-casting.md\\\\n */\\\\n\\\\nimport { Context, Effect, Layer, pipe } from 'effect'\\\\nimport type { ConfigError } from 'effect/ConfigError'\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Pattern 1: Simple mock with static values\\\\n */\\\\nexport const createSimpleMock = <T>(\\\\n  tag: Context.Tag<T, T>,\\\\n  implementation: T\\\\n): Layer.Layer<T> => {\\\\n  return Layer.succeed(tag, implementation)\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Pattern 2: Mock with configurable behavior\\\\n */\\\\nexport interface MockConfig<T> {\\\\n  implementation: Partial<T>\\\\n  delays?: Record<string, number>\\\\n  errors?: Record<string, Error>\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\nexport const createConfigurableMock = <T extends Record<string, any>>(\\\\n  tag: Context.Tag<T, T>,\\\\n  config: MockConfig<T>\\\\n): Layer.Layer<T> => {\\\\n  const implementation = {} as T\\\\n  \\\\n  for (const key in config.implementation) {\\\\n    const method = config.implementation[key]\\\\n    if (typeof method === 'function') {\\\\n      implementation[key] = ((...args: any[]) => {\\\\n        // Add configured delay\\\\n        const delay = config.delays?.[key] || 0\\\\n        \\\\n        // Check for configured error\\\\n        const error = config.errors?.[key]\\\\n        \\\\n        return pipe(\\\\n          Effect.sleep(delay),\\\\n          Effect.flatMap(() => \\\\n            error \\\\n              ? Effect.fail(error)\\\\n              : Effect.succeed(method(...args))\\\\n          )\\\\n        )\\\\n      }) as any\\\\n    } else {\\\\n      implementation[key] = method as any\\\\n    }\\\\n  }\\\\n  \\\\n  return Layer.succeed(tag, implementation)\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Pattern 3: Spy mock that records calls\\\\n */\\\\nexport interface SpyMock<T> {\\\\n  implementation: T\\\\n  calls: Record<string, any[][]>\\\\n  resetCalls: () => void\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\nexport const createSpyMock = <T extends Record<string, any>>(\\\\n  tag: Context.Tag<T, T>,\\\\n  baseImplementation: T\\\\n): Layer.Layer<T> & { spy: SpyMock<T> } => {\\\\n  const calls: Record<string, any[][]> = {}\\\\n  \\\\n  const implementation = {} as T\\\\n  \\\\n  for (const key in baseImplementation) {\\\\n    const method = baseImplementation[key]\\\\n    if (typeof method === 'function') {\\\\n      calls[key] = []\\\\n      implementation[key] = ((...args: any[]) => {\\\\n        calls[key].push(args)\\\\n        return method(...args)\\\\n      }) as any\\\\n    } else {\\\\n      implementation[key] = method\\\\n    }\\\\n  }\\\\n  \\\\n  const spy: SpyMock<T> = {\\\\n    implementation,\\\\n    calls,\\\\n    resetCalls: () => {\\\\n      for (const key in calls) {\\\\n        calls[key] = []\\\\n      }\\\\n    }\\\\n  }\\\\n  \\\\n  const layer = Layer.succeed(tag, implementation) as Layer.Layer<T> & { spy: SpyMock<T> }\\\\n  layer.spy = spy\\\\n  \\\\n  return layer\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Pattern 4: State-based mock\\\\n */\\\\nexport interface StatefulMock<T, S> {\\\\n  implementation: T\\\\n  getState: () => S\\\\n  setState: (state: S) => void\\\\n  resetState: () => void\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\nexport const createStatefulMock = <T extends Record<string, any>, S>(\\\\n  tag: Context.Tag<T, T>,\\\\n  initialState: S,\\\\n  implementationFactory: (\\\\n    getState: () => S,\\\\n    setState: (state: S) => void\\\\n  ) => T\\\\n): Layer.Layer<T> & { mock: StatefulMock<T, S> } => {\\\\n  let state = initialState\\\\n  \\\\n  const getState = () => state\\\\n  const setState = (newState: S) => { state = newState }\\\\n  const resetState = () => { state = initialState }\\\\n  \\\\n  const implementation = implementationFactory(getState, setState)\\\\n  \\\\n  const mock: StatefulMock<T, S> = {\\\\n    implementation,\\\\n    getState,\\\\n    setState,\\\\n    resetState\\\\n  }\\\\n  \\\\n  const layer = Layer.succeed(tag, implementation) as Layer.Layer<T> & { mock: StatefulMock<T, S> }\\\\n  layer.mock = mock\\\\n  \\\\n  return layer\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Pattern 5: Async mock with promise resolution control\\\\n */\\\\nexport interface AsyncMock<T> {\\\\n  implementation: T\\\\n  resolvers: Record<string, {\\\\n    resolve: (value: any) => void\\\\n    reject: (error: any) => void\\\\n  }[]>\\\\n  waitForCall: (method: string, callIndex?: number) => Promise<void>\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\nexport const createAsyncMock = <T extends Record<string, any>>(\\\\n  tag: Context.Tag<T, T>,\\\\n  methods: (keyof T)[]\\\\n): Layer.Layer<T> & { async: AsyncMock<T> } => {\\\\n  const resolvers: Record<string, any[]> = {}\\\\n  const pendingCalls: Record<string, Promise<void>[]> = {}\\\\n  \\\\n  const implementation = {} as T\\\\n  \\\\n  for (const method of methods) {\\\\n    resolvers[method as string] = []\\\\n    pendingCalls[method as string] = []\\\\n    \\\\n    implementation[method as string] = (() => {\\\\n      return Effect.async((resume) => {\\\\n        const promise = new Promise<void>((resolve) => {\\\\n          const resolverObj = {\\\\n            resolve: (value: any) => {\\\\n              resume(Effect.succeed(value))\\\\n              resolve()\\\\n            },\\\\n            reject: (error: any) => {\\\\n              resume(Effect.fail(error))\\\\n              resolve()\\\\n            }\\\\n          }\\\\n          resolvers[method as string].push(resolverObj)\\\\n        })\\\\n        pendingCalls[method as string].push(promise)\\\\n      })\\\\n    }) as any\\\\n  }\\\\n  \\\\n  const asyncMock: AsyncMock<T> = {\\\\n    implementation,\\\\n    resolvers: resolvers as any,\\\\n    waitForCall: async (method: string, callIndex = 0) => {\\\\n      const calls = pendingCalls[method]\\\\n      if (calls && calls[callIndex]) {\\\\n        await calls[callIndex]\\\\n      }\\\\n    }\\\\n  }\\\\n  \\\\n  const layer = Layer.succeed(tag, implementation) as Layer.Layer<T> & { async: AsyncMock<T> }\\\\n  layer.async = asyncMock\\\\n  \\\\n  return layer\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Example usage with a hypothetical UserService\\\\n */\\\\nexport interface UserService {\\\\n  getUser: (id: string) => Effect.Effect<{ id: string; name: string }, Error>\\\\n  updateUser: (id: string, name: string) => Effect.Effect<void, Error>\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\nexport const UserService = Context.GenericTag<UserService>('UserService')\\\\n\\\\n// Example 1: Simple mock\\\\nexport const UserServiceSimpleMock = createSimpleMock(UserService, {\\\\n  getUser: (id: string) => Effect.succeed({ id, name: 'Test User' }),\\\\n  updateUser: () => Effect.succeed(undefined)\\\\n})\\\\n\\\\n// Example 2: Spy mock\\\\nexport const UserServiceSpyMock = createSpyMock(UserService, {\\\\n  getUser: (id: string) => Effect.succeed({ id, name: 'Test User' }),\\\\n  updateUser: () => Effect.succeed(undefined)\\\\n})\\\\n\\\\n// Example 3: Stateful mock\\\\nexport const UserServiceStatefulMock = createStatefulMock(\\\\n  UserService,\\\\n  { users: new Map<string, string>() },\\\\n  (getState, setState) => ({\\\\n    getUser: (id: string) =>\\\\n      Effect.gen(function* () {\\\\n        const state = getState()\\\\n        const name = state.users.get(id)\\\\n        if (!name) {\\\\n          return yield* Effect.fail(new Error('User not found'))\\\\n        }\\\\n        return { id, name }\\\\n      }),\\\\n    updateUser: (id: string, name: string) =>\\\\n      Effect.sync(() => {\\\\n        const state = getState()\\\\n        state.users.set(id, name)\\\\n        setState(state)\\\\n      })\\\\n  })\\\\n)\\\\n\\\\n// Test helpers\\\\nexport const runEffectTest = async <A, E>(\\\\n  effect: Effect.Effect<A, E, any>,\\\\n  layer: Layer.Layer<any>\\\\n): Promise<A> => {\\\\n  return Effect.runPromise(pipe(effect, Effect.provide(layer)))\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\nexport const runEffectTestExpectError = async <A, E>(\\\\n  effect: Effect.Effect<A, E, any>,\\\\n  layer: Layer.Layer<any>\\\\n): Promise<E> => {\\\\n  try {\\\\n    await Effect.runPromise(pipe(effect, Effect.provide(layer)))\\\\n    throw new Error('Expected effect to fail')\\\\n  } catch (error) {\\\\n    return error as E\\\\n  }\\\\n}\\\"}\"}},{\"id\":\"toolu_01GY7PDGqioqpmFZAhLTSmzd\",\"type\":\"function\",\"function\":{\"name\":\"Write\",\"arguments\":\"{\\\"file_path\\\":\\\"/Users/christopherdavid/code/commander/src/services/patterns/EffectCheatSheet.md\\\",\\\"content\\\":\\\"# Effect-TS Cheat Sheet for OpenAgents Commander\\\\n\\\\nThis cheat sheet covers common Effect patterns and solutions to issues found in docs/fixes/.\\\\n\\\\n## Table of Contents\\\\n1. [Service Creation Patterns](#service-creation-patterns)\\\\n2. [Error Handling](#error-handling)  \\\\n3. [Configuration Management](#configuration-management)\\\\n4. [Testing Patterns](#testing-patterns)\\\\n5. [React Integration](#react-integration)\\\\n6. [Common Gotchas](#common-gotchas)\\\\n\\\\n## Service Creation Patterns\\\\n\\\\n### ✅ DO: Use Context.GenericTag\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// Correct - provides better type inference\\\\nexport const MyService = Context.GenericTag<MyService>('MyService')\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### ❌ DON'T: Use Context.Tag\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// Incorrect - can cause type inference issues\\\\nexport const MyService = Context.Tag<MyService>('MyService')\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### ✅ DO: Create specific error types\\\\n```typescript\\\\nexport class MyServiceError extends Error {\\\\n  readonly _tag = 'MyServiceError'\\\\n  constructor(message: string, readonly cause?: unknown) {\\\\n    super(message)\\\\n  }\\\\n}\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### ✅ DO: Use Layer.effect for complex services\\\\n```typescript\\\\nexport const MyServiceLive = Layer.effect(\\\\n  MyService,\\\\n  Effect.gen(function* () {\\\\n    const config = yield* ConfigService\\\\n    const database = yield* DatabaseService\\\\n    return new MyServiceImpl(config, database)\\\\n  })\\\\n)\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n## Error Handling\\\\n\\\\n### Handle different error types\\\\n```typescript\\\\nEffect.catchTags({\\\\n  ConfigError: (error) => Effect.fail(new MyServiceError('Invalid config', error)),\\\\n  NetworkError: (error) => Effect.fail(new MyServiceError('Network failed', error)),\\\\n  // Catch-all for unexpected errors\\\\n  UnknownException: (error) => Effect.fail(new MyServiceError('Unknown error', error))\\\\n})\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### Convert Promise errors safely\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// ✅ DO: Wrap promises properly\\\\nEffect.tryPromise({\\\\n  try: () => fetch(url),\\\\n  catch: (error) => new MyServiceError('Fetch failed', error)\\\\n})\\\\n\\\\n// ❌ DON'T: Use Effect.promise directly\\\\nEffect.promise(() => fetch(url)) // Loses error context\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n## Configuration Management\\\\n\\\\n### ✅ DO: Forbid fallbacks for sensitive data (Fix #022)\\\\n```typescript\\\\nexport const ApiKeyConfig = Config.string('API_KEY').pipe(\\\\n  Config.validate({\\\\n    message: 'API_KEY is required',\\\\n    validation: (key) => key.length > 0\\\\n  })\\\\n)\\\\n\\\\n// ❌ DON'T: Use withDefault for secrets\\\\nConfig.string('API_KEY').pipe(Config.withDefault('test-key'))\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### ✅ DO: Create typed configuration services\\\\n```typescript\\\\nexport interface MyServiceConfig {\\\\n  readonly apiUrl: string\\\\n  readonly timeout: number\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\nexport const MyServiceConfig = Context.GenericTag<MyServiceConfig>('MyServiceConfig')\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n## Testing Patterns\\\\n\\\\n### Use test layers properly\\\\n```typescript\\\\ndescribe('MyService', () => {\\\\n  const testLayer = Layer.mergeAll(\\\\n    DatabaseServiceTest,\\\\n    ConfigServiceTest,\\\\n    MyServiceLive\\\\n  )\\\\n  \\\\n  it('should work', async () => {\\\\n    const result = await Effect.runPromise(\\\\n      myServiceMethod.pipe(Effect.provide(testLayer))\\\\n    )\\\\n    expect(result).toBe(expected)\\\\n  })\\\\n})\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### Mock with spy capabilities\\\\n```typescript\\\\nconst mockLayer = createSpyMock(MyService, {\\\\n  doSomething: () => Effect.succeed('mocked')\\\\n})\\\\n\\\\n// After test\\\\nexpect(mockLayer.spy.calls.doSomething).toHaveLength(1)\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n## React Integration\\\\n\\\\n### ✅ DO: Use refs to prevent stale closures (Fix #023)\\\\n```typescript\\\\nconst runtimeRef = useRef(runtime)\\\\nuseEffect(() => {\\\\n  runtimeRef.current = runtime\\\\n}, [runtime])\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### ✅ DO: Cleanup Effect fibers\\\\n```typescript\\\\nuseEffect(() => {\\\\n  const fiber = Effect.runFork(myEffect)(runtime)\\\\n  return () => {\\\\n    fiber.interrupt\\\\n  }\\\\n}, [deps])\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### ❌ DON'T: Create runtime in render\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// Bad - creates new runtime every render\\\\nconst runtime = Runtime.make(layer)\\\\n\\\\n// Good - use useEffectRuntime hook\\\\nconst runtime = useEffectRuntime(layer)\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n## Common Gotchas\\\\n\\\\n### 1. Double-yield in Layer.effect (Fix #014)\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// ❌ Wrong - double yield\\\\nLayer.effect(\\\\n  MyService,\\\\n  Effect.gen(function* () {\\\\n    return yield* Effect.succeed(new MyServiceImpl())\\\\n  })\\\\n)\\\\n\\\\n// ✅ Correct - single yield or direct return\\\\nLayer.effect(\\\\n  MyService,\\\\n  Effect.gen(function* () {\\\\n    const config = yield* ConfigService\\\\n    return new MyServiceImpl(config)\\\\n  })\\\\n)\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### 2. Service dependency cycles\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// ❌ Circular dependency\\\\nconst ServiceA = Layer.effect(ServiceATag, \\\\n  Effect.gen(function* () {\\\\n    const b = yield* ServiceB // ServiceB depends on ServiceA!\\\\n  })\\\\n)\\\\n\\\\n// ✅ Use factory pattern or restructure\\\\nconst ServiceAFactory = (serviceB: ServiceB) => \\\\n  Layer.succeed(ServiceATag, new ServiceAImpl(serviceB))\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### 3. Effect vs Promise in APIs\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// When exposing to non-Effect code\\\\nexport class MyServiceBridge {\\\\n  async doSomething(input: string): Promise<Result> {\\\\n    return Effect.runPromise(\\\\n      this.service.doSomething(input).pipe(\\\\n        Effect.provide(this.runtime)\\\\n      )\\\\n    )\\\\n  }\\\\n}\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### 4. Type inference with pipe\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// Help TypeScript with explicit types when needed\\\\npipe(\\\\n  myEffect,\\\\n  Effect.map((value): ProcessedType => processValue(value)),\\\\n  Effect.catchAll((error): Effect.Effect<Fallback, never> => \\\\n    Effect.succeed(fallbackValue)\\\\n  )\\\\n)\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### 5. Testing async Effects\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// Use Effect.runPromise in tests\\\\nit('should handle async operations', async () => {\\\\n  const result = await Effect.runPromise(\\\\n    myAsyncEffect.pipe(Effect.provide(testLayer))\\\\n  )\\\\n  expect(result).toBe(expected)\\\\n})\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n## Quick Reference\\\\n\\\\n### Essential imports\\\\n```typescript\\\\nimport { Context, Effect, Layer, pipe, Stream } from 'effect'\\\\nimport * as Config from 'effect/Config'\\\\nimport * as Runtime from 'effect/Runtime'\\\\nimport * as Exit from 'effect/Exit'\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### Service lifecycle\\\\n1. Define interface → 2. Create Tag → 3. Implement → 4. Create Layer → 5. Compose\\\\n\\\\n### Effect operators by use case\\\\n- **Transform**: `map`, `flatMap`, `tap`\\\\n- **Error handling**: `catchAll`, `catchTags`, `orElse`\\\\n- **Resource management**: `acquireRelease`, `scoped`\\\\n- **Concurrency**: `all`, `race`, `forEach`\\\\n- **Retry**: `retry`, `retryOrElse`\\\\n- **Timeout**: `timeout`, `timeoutFail`\\\\n\\\\n### Debug helpers\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// Log intermediate values\\\\nEffect.tap(value => Effect.log('Value:', value))\\\\n\\\\n// Log and pass through\\\\nEffect.tapBoth({\\\\n  onFailure: (error) => Effect.log('Error:', error),\\\\n  onSuccess: (value) => Effect.log('Success:', value)\\\\n})\\\\n```\\\"}\"}}]","timestamp":1748307521}}

super(message)\\\\n    this.name = 'ExampleServiceError'\\\\n  }\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Step 2: Define your service interface\\\\n * This is what consumers will interact with\\\\n */\\\\nexport interface ExampleService {\\\\n  readonly doSomething: (input: string) => Effect.Effect<string, ExampleServiceError>\\\\n  readonly doSomethingAsync: (input: number) => Effect.Effect<number, ExampleServiceError>\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Step 3: Create the service Tag\\\\n * IMPORTANT: Use Context.GenericTag, not Context.Tag (fixes type inference issues)\\\\n */\\\\nexport const ExampleService = Context.GenericTag<ExampleService>('ExampleService')\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Step 4: Define configuration interface if needed\\\\n */\\\\nexport interface ExampleServiceConfig {\\\\n  readonly apiUrl: string\\\\n  readonly timeout: number\\\\n  readonly enableDebug?: boolean\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Step 5: Create configuration service tag\\\\n */\\\\nexport const ExampleServiceConfig = Context.GenericTag<ExampleServiceConfig>('ExampleServiceConfig')\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Step 6: Implement the service\\\\n * Use a class for complex services, object literal for simple ones\\\\n */\\\\nexport class ExampleServiceImpl implements ExampleService {\\\\n  constructor(\\\\n    private readonly config: ExampleServiceConfig\\\\n  ) {}\\\\n\\\\n  readonly doSomething = (input: string) =>\\\\n    Effect.gen(function* () {\\\\n      try {\\\\n        // Your implementation here\\\\n        if (input.length === 0) {\\\\n          return yield* Effect.fail(\\\\n            new ExampleServiceError('Input cannot be empty')\\\\n          )\\\\n        }\\\\n        \\\\n        if (this.config.enableDebug) {\\\\n          yield* Effect.log(`Processing: ${input}`)\\\\n        }\\\\n        \\\\n        return `Processed: ${input}`\\\\n      } catch (error) {\\\\n        return yield* Effect.fail(\\\\n          new ExampleServiceError('Failed to process input', error)\\\\n        )\\\\n      }\\\\n    })\\\\n\\\\n  readonly doSomethingAsync = (input: number) =>\\\\n    Effect.gen(function* () {\\\\n      try {\\\\n        // Simulate async operation\\\\n        yield* Effect.sleep(100)\\\\n        \\\\n        if (input < 0) {\\\\n          return yield* Effect.fail(\\\\n            new ExampleServiceError('Input must be positive')\\\\n          )\\\\n        }\\\\n        \\\\n        // Example of calling external API with timeout\\\\n        const result = yield* pipe(\\\\n          Effect.promise(() => \\\\n            fetch(`${this.config.apiUrl}/data/${input}`)\\\\n              .then(res => res.json())\\\\n              .then(data => data.value as number)\\\\n          ),\\\\n          Effect.timeout(this.config.timeout),\\\\n          Effect.catchAll((error) => \\\\n            Effect.fail(new ExampleServiceError('API call failed', error))\\\\n          )\\\\n        )\\\\n        \\\\n        return result\\\\n      } catch (error) {\\\\n        return yield* Effect.fail(\\\\n          new ExampleServiceError('Async operation failed', error)\\\\n        )\\\\n      }\\\\n    })\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Step 7: Create the service Layer\\\\n * This is how the service is provided to the application\\\\n */\\\\nexport const ExampleServiceLive = Layer.effect(\\\\n  ExampleService,\\\\n  Effect.gen(function* () {\\\\n    const config = yield* ExampleServiceConfig\\\\n    return new ExampleServiceImpl(config)\\\\n  })\\\\n)\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Step 8: Create a * Effect React Hooks\\\\n * \\\\n * Safe patterns for using Effect services in React components\\\\n * Addresses issues from docs/fixes/023-effect-runtime-stale-references.md\\\\n */\\\\n\\\\nimport { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'\\\\nimport { Effect, Exit, Runtime, pipe } from 'effect'\\\\nimport type { Context } from 'effect'\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Hook for using Effect services in React components\\\\n * Prevents stale reference issues by properly managing the runtime lifecycle\\\\n */\\\\nexport function useEffectService<A, E, R>(\\\\n  effect: Effect.Effect<A, E, R>,\\\\n  runtime: Runtime.Runtime<R>,\\\\n  deps: React.DependencyList = []\\\\n): {\\\\n  data: A | null\\\\n  error: E | null\\\\n  loading: boolean\\\\n  execute: () => void\\\\n} {\\\\n  const [data, setData] = useState<A | null>(null)\\\\n  const [error, setError] = useState<E | null>(null)\\\\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)\\\\n  \\\\n  // Use ref to store the latest runtime to prevent stale closures\\\\n  const runtimeRef = useRef(runtime)\\\\n  useEffect(() => {\\\\n    runtimeRef.current = runtime\\\\n  }, [runtime])\\\\n  \\\\n  // Store abort controller to cancel in-flight requests\\\\n  const abortControllerRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null)\\\\n  \\\\n  const execute = useCallback(() => {\\\\n    // Cancel any in-flight request\\\\n    if (abortControllerRef.current) {\\\\n      abortControllerRef.current.abort()\\\\n    }\\\\n    \\\\n    const abortController = new AbortController()\\\\n    abortControllerRef.current = abortController\\\\n    \\\\n    setLoading(true)\\\\n    setError(null)\\\\n    \\\\n    const fiber = Effect.runFork(effect, {\\\\n      signal: abortController.signal\\\\n    })(runtimeRef.current)\\\\n    \\\\n    fiber.await.then((exit) => {\\\\n      if (!abortController.signal.aborted) {\\\\n        if (Exit.isSuccess(exit)) {\\\\n          setData(exit.value)\\\\n          setError(null)\\\\n        } else {\\\\n          setData(null)\\\\n          setError(exit.cause as unknown as E)\\\\n        }\\\\n        setLoading(false)\\\\n      }\\\\n    })\\\\n    \\\\n    return () => {\\\\n      abortController.abort()\\\\n    }\\\\n  }, [...deps, effect])\\\\n  \\\\n  // Cleanup on unmount\\\\n  useEffect(() => {\\\\n    return () => {\\\\n      if (abortControllerRef.current) {\\\\n        abortControllerRef.current.abort()\\\\n      }\\\\n    }\\\\n  }, [])\\\\n  \\\\n  return { data, error, loading, execute }\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Hook for using Effect services with automatic execution\\\\n */\\\\nexport function useEffectServiceAuto<A, E, R>(\\\\n  effect: Effect.Effect<A, E, R>,\\\\n  runtime: Runtime.Runtime<R>,\\\\n  deps: React.DependencyList = []\\\\n) {\\\\n  const result = useEffectService(effect, runtime, deps)\\\\n  \\\\n  useEffect(() => {\\\\n    result.execute()\\\\n  }, [result.execute])\\\\n  \\\\n  return result\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Hook for creating a stable Effect runtime in React\\\\n * Prevents recreation on every render\\\\n */\\\\nexport function useEffectRuntime<R>(\\\\n  layer: Effect.Layer<R, never, never>,\\\\n  deps: React.DependencyList = []\\\\n): Runtime.Runtime<R> | null {\\\\n  const [runtime, setRuntime] = useState<Runtime.Runtime<R> | null>(null)\\\\n  \\\\n  useEffect(() => {\\\\n    let canceled = false\\\\n    \\\\n    Effect.runPromise(\\\\n      pipe(\\\\n        Effect.runtime<R>(),\\\\n        Effect.provide(layer)\\\\n      )\\\\n    ).then((rt) => {\\\\n      if (!canceled) {\\\\n        setRuntime(rt)\\\\n      }\\\\n    })\\\\n    \\\\n    return () => {\\\\n      canceled = true\\\\n    }\\\\n  }, deps)\\\\n  \\\\n  return runtime\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Hook for accessing a specific service from the runtime\\\\n */\\\\nexport function useService<T>(\\\\n  tag: Context.Tag<T, T>,\\\\n  runtime: Runtime.Runtime<any> | null\\\\n): T | null {\\\\n  const [service, setService] = useState<T | null>(null)\\\\n  \\\\n  useEffect(() => {\\\\n    if (runtime) {\\\\n      const context = runtime.context\\\\n      if (Context.isContext(context)) {\\\\n        const serviceInstance = Context.getOption(context, tag)\\\\n        if (serviceInstance._tag === 'Some') {\\\\n          setService(serviceInstance.value)\\\\n        }\\\\n      }\\\\n    }\\\\n  }, [runtime, tag])\\\\n  \\\\n  return service\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Hook for streaming Effect values\\\\n * Useful for real-time updates, progress tracking, etc.\\\\n */\\\\nexport function useEffectStream<A, E, R>(\\\\n  stream: Effect.Stream<A, E, R>,\\\\n  runtime: Runtime.Runtime<R>,\\\\n  onValue: (value: A) => void,\\\\n  onError?: (error: E) => void,\\\\n  deps: React.DependencyList = []\\\\n) {\\\\n  const runtimeRef = useRef(runtime)\\\\n  useEffect(() => {\\\\n    runtimeRef.current = runtime\\\\n  }, [runtime])\\\\n  \\\\n  useEffect(() => {\\\\n    const fiber = Effect.runFork(\\\\n      stream.pipe(\\\\n        Effect.tap((value) => Effect.sync(() => onValue(value))),\\\\n        Effect.catchAll((error) =>\\\\n          Effect.sync(() => {\\\\n            if (onError) onError(error)\\\\n          })\\\\n        )\\\\n      )\\\\n    )(runtimeRef.current)\\\\n    \\\\n    return () => {\\\\n      fiber.interrupt\\\\n    }\\\\n  }, deps)\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Error boundary component for Effect services\\\\n */\\\\nexport class EffectErrorBoundary extends React.Component<\\\\n  {\\\\n    children: React.ReactNode\\\\n    fallback: (error: unknown) => React.ReactNode\\\\n  },\\\\n  { hasError: boolean; error: unknown }\\\\n> {\\\\n  constructor(props: any) {\\\\n    super(props)\\\\n    this.state = { hasError: false, error: null }\\\\n  }\\\\n  \\\\n  static getDerivedStateFromError(error: unknown) {\\\\n    return { hasError: true, error }\\\\n  }\\\\n  \\\\n  componentDidCatch(error: Error, errorInfo: React.ErrorInfo) {\\\\n    console.error('Effect service error:', error, errorInfo)\\\\n  }\\\\n  \\\\n  render() {\\\\n    if (this.state.hasError) {\\\\n      return this.props.fallback(this.state.error)\\\\n    }\\\\n    \\\\n    return this.props.children\\\\n  }\\\\n}\\\"}\"}},{\"id\":\"toolu_0175Q5ugorSTdZsn7VptP19j\",\"type\":\"function\",\"function\":{\"name\":\"Write\",\"arguments\":\"{\\\"file_path\\\":\\\"/Users/christopherdavid/code/commander/src/services/patterns/TestServiceMocks.ts\\\",\\\"content\\\":\\\"/**\\\\n * Test Service Mocks Template\\\\n * \\\\n * Patterns for creating test mocks that satisfy Effect's type system\\\\n * Addresses issues from docs/fixes/012-strategic-test-type-casting.md\\\\n */\\\\n\\\\nimport { Context, Effect, Layer, pipe } from 'effect'\\\\nimport type { ConfigError } from 'effect/ConfigError'\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Pattern 1: Simple mock with static values\\\\n */\\\\nexport const createSimpleMock = <T>(\\\\n  tag: Context.Tag<T, T>,\\\\n  implementation: T\\\\n): Layer.Layer<T> => {\\\\n  return Layer.succeed(tag, implementation)\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Pattern 2: Mock with configurable behavior\\\\n */\\\\nexport interface MockConfig<T> {\\\\n  implementation: Partial<T>\\\\n  delays?: Record<string, number>\\\\n  errors?: Record<string, Error>\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\nexport const createConfigurableMock = <T extends Record<string, any>>(\\\\n  tag: Context.Tag<T, T>,\\\\n  config: MockConfig<T>\\\\n): Layer.Layer<T> => {\\\\n  const implementation = {} as T\\\\n  \\\\n  for (const key in config.implementation) {\\\\n    const method = config.implementation[key]\\\\n    if (typeof method === 'function') {\\\\n      implementation[key] = ((...args: any[]) => {\\\\n        // Add configured delay\\\\n        const delay = config.delays?.[key] || 0\\\\n        \\\\n        // Check for configured error\\\\n        const error = config.errors?.[key]\\\\n        \\\\n        return pipe(\\\\n          Effect.sleep(delay),\\\\n          Effect.flatMap(() => \\\\n            error \\\\n              ? Effect.fail(error)\\\\n              : Effect.succeed(method(...args))\\\\n          )\\\\n        )\\\\n      }) as any\\\\n    } else {\\\\n      implementation[key] = method as any\\\\n    }\\\\n  }\\\\n  \\\\n  return Layer.succeed(tag, implementation)\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Pattern 3: Spy mock that records calls\\\\n */\\\\nexport interface SpyMock<T> {\\\\n  implementation: T\\\\n  calls: Record<string, any[][]>\\\\n  resetCalls: () => void\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\nexport const createSpyMock = <T extends Record<string, any>>(\\\\n  tag: Context.Tag<T, T>,\\\\n  baseImplementation: T\\\\n): Layer.Layer<T> & { spy: SpyMock<T> } => {\\\\n  const calls: Record<string, any[][]> = {}\\\\n  \\\\n  const implementation = {} as T\\\\n  \\\\n  for (const key in baseImplementation) {\\\\n    const method = baseImplementation[key]\\\\n    if (typeof method === 'function') {\\\\n      calls[key] = []\\\\n      implementation[key] = ((...args: any[]) => {\\\\n        calls[key].push(args)\\\\n        return method(...args)\\\\n      }) as any\\\\n    } else {\\\\n      implementation[key] = method\\\\n    }\\\\n  }\\\\n  \\\\n  const spy: SpyMock<T> = {\\\\n    implementation,\\\\n    calls,\\\\n    resetCalls: () => {\\\\n      for (const key in calls) {\\\\n        calls[key] = []\\\\n      }\\\\n    }\\\\n  }\\\\n  \\\\n  const layer = Layer.succeed(tag, implementation) as Layer.Layer<T> & { spy: SpyMock<T> }\\\\n  layer.spy = spy\\\\n  \\\\n  return layer\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Pattern 4: State-based mock\\\\n */\\\\nexport interface StatefulMock<T, S> {\\\\n  implementation: T\\\\n  getState: () => S\\\\n  setState: (state: S) => void\\\\n  resetState: () => void\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\nexport const createStatefulMock = <T extends Record<string, any>, S>(\\\\n  tag: Context.Tag<T, T>,\\\\n  initialState: S,\\\\n  implementationFactory: (\\\\n    getState: () => S,\\\\n    setState: (state: S) => void\\\\n  ) => T\\\\n): Layer.Layer<T> & { mock: StatefulMock<T, S> } => {\\\\n  let state = initialState\\\\n  \\\\n  const getState = () => state\\\\n  const setState = (newState: S) => { state = newState }\\\\n  const resetState = () => { state = initialState }\\\\n  \\\\n  const implementation = implementationFactory(getState, setState)\\\\n  \\\\n  const mock: StatefulMock<T, S> = {\\\\n    implementation,\\\\n    getState,\\\\n    setState,\\\\n    resetState\\\\n  }\\\\n  \\\\n  const layer = Layer.succeed(tag, implementation) as Layer.Layer<T> & { mock: StatefulMock<T, S> }\\\\n  layer.mock = mock\\\\n  \\\\n  return layer\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Pattern 5: Async mock with promise resolution control\\\\n */\\\\nexport interface AsyncMock<T> {\\\\n  implementation: T\\\\n  resolvers: Record<string, {\\\\n    resolve: (value: any) => void\\\\n    reject: (error: any) => void\\\\n  }[]>\\\\n  waitForCall: (method: string, callIndex?: number) => Promise<void>\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\nexport const createAsyncMock = <T extends Record<string, any>>(\\\\n  tag: Context.Tag<T, T>,\\\\n  methods: (keyof T)[]\\\\n): Layer.Layer<T> & { async: AsyncMock<T> } => {\\\\n  const resolvers: Record<string, any[]> = {}\\\\n  const pendingCalls: Record<string, Promise<void>[]> = {}\\\\n  \\\\n  const implementation = {} as T\\\\n  \\\\n  for (const method of methods) {\\\\n    resolvers[method as string] = []\\\\n    pendingCalls[method as string] = []\\\\n    \\\\n    implementation[method as string] = (() => {\\\\n      return Effect.async((resume) => {\\\\n        const promise = new Promise<void>((resolve) => {\\\\n          const resolverObj = {\\\\n            resolve: (value: any) => {\\\\n              resume(Effect.succeed(value))\\\\n              resolve()\\\\n            },\\\\n            reject: (error: any) => {\\\\n              resume(Effect.fail(error))\\\\n              resolve()\\\\n            }\\\\n          }\\\\n          resolvers[method as string].push(resolverObj)\\\\n        })\\\\n        pendingCalls[method as string].push(promise)\\\\n      })\\\\n    }) as any\\\\n  }\\\\n  \\\\n  const asyncMock: AsyncMock<T> = {\\\\n    implementation,\\\\n    resolvers: resolvers as any,\\\\n    waitForCall: async (method: string, callIndex = 0) => {\\\\n      const calls = pendingCalls[method]\\\\n      if (calls && calls[callIndex]) {\\\\n        await calls[callIndex]\\\\n      }\\\\n    }\\\\n  }\\\\n  \\\\n  const layer = Layer.succeed(tag, implementation) as Layer.Layer<T> & { async: AsyncMock<T> }\\\\n  layer.async = asyncMock\\\\n  \\\\n  return layer\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n/**\\\\n * Example usage with a hypothetical UserService\\\\n */\\\\nexport interface UserService {\\\\n  getUser: (id: string) => Effect.Effect<{ id: string; name: string }, Error>\\\\n  updateUser: (id: string, name: string) => Effect.Effect<void, Error>\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\nexport const UserService = Context.GenericTag<UserService>('UserService')\\\\n\\\\n// Example 1: Simple mock\\\\nexport const UserServiceSimpleMock = createSimpleMock(UserService, {\\\\n  getUser: (id: string) => Effect.succeed({ id, name: 'Test User' }),\\\\n  updateUser: () => Effect.succeed(undefined)\\\\n})\\\\n\\\\n// Example 2: Spy mock\\\\nexport const UserServiceSpyMock = createSpyMock(UserService, {\\\\n  getUser: (id: string) => Effect.succeed({ id, name: 'Test User' }),\\\\n  updateUser: () => Effect.succeed(undefined)\\\\n})\\\\n\\\\n// Example 3: Stateful mock\\\\nexport const UserServiceStatefulMock = createStatefulMock(\\\\n  UserService,\\\\n  { users: new Map<string, string>() },\\\\n  (getState, setState) => ({\\\\n    getUser: (id: string) =>\\\\n      Effect.gen(function* () {\\\\n        const state = getState()\\\\n        const name = state.users.get(id)\\\\n        if (!name) {\\\\n          return yield* Effect.fail(new Error('User not found'))\\\\n        }\\\\n        return { id, name }\\\\n      }),\\\\n    updateUser: (id: string, name: string) =>\\\\n      Effect.sync(() => {\\\\n        const state = getState()\\\\n        state.users.set(id, name)\\\\n        setState(state)\\\\n      })\\\\n  })\\\\n)\\\\n\\\\n// Test helpers\\\\nexport const runEffectTest = async <A, E>(\\\\n  effect: Effect.Effect<A, E, any>,\\\\n  layer: Layer.Layer<any>\\\\n): Promise<A> => {\\\\n  return Effect.runPromise(pipe(effect, Effect.provide(layer)))\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\nexport const runEffectTestExpectError = async <A, E>(\\\\n  effect: Effect.Effect<A, E, any>,\\\\n  layer: Layer.Layer<any>\\\\n): Promise<E> => {\\\\n  try {\\\\n    await Effect.runPromise(pipe(effect, Effect.provide(layer)))\\\\n    throw new Error('Expected effect to fail')\\\\n  } catch (error) {\\\\n    return error as E\\\\n  }\\\\n}\\\"}\"}},{\"id\":\"toolu_01GY7PDGqioqpmFZAhLTSmzd\",\"type\":\"function\",\"function\":{\"name\":\"Write\",\"arguments\":\"{\\\"file_path\\\":\\\"/Users/christopherdavid/code/commander/src/services/patterns/EffectCheatSheet.md\\\",\\\"content\\\":\\\"# Effect-TS Cheat Sheet for OpenAgents Commander\\\\n\\\\nThis cheat sheet covers common Effect patterns and solutions to issues found in docs/fixes/.\\\\n\\\\n## Table of Contents\\\\n1. [Service Creation Patterns](#service-creation-patterns)\\\\n2. [Error Handling](#error-handling)  \\\\n3. [Configuration Management](#configuration-management)\\\\n4. [Testing Patterns](#testing-patterns)\\\\n5. [React Integration](#react-integration)\\\\n6. [Common Gotchas](#common-gotchas)\\\\n\\\\n## Service Creation Patterns\\\\n\\\\n### ✅ DO: Use Context.GenericTag\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// Correct - provides better type inference\\\\nexport const MyService = Context.GenericTag<MyService>('MyService')\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### ❌ DON'T: Use Context.Tag\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// Incorrect - can cause type inference issues\\\\nexport const MyService = Context.Tag<MyService>('MyService')\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### ✅ DO: Create specific error types\\\\n```typescript\\\\nexport class MyServiceError extends Error {\\\\n  readonly _tag = 'MyServiceError'\\\\n  constructor(message: string, readonly cause?: unknown) {\\\\n    super(message)\\\\n  }\\\\n}\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### ✅ DO: Use Layer.effect for complex services\\\\n```typescript\\\\nexport const MyServiceLive = Layer.effect(\\\\n  MyService,\\\\n  Effect.gen(function* () {\\\\n    const config = yield* ConfigService\\\\n    const database = yield* DatabaseService\\\\n    return new MyServiceImpl(config, database)\\\\n  })\\\\n)\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n## Error Handling\\\\n\\\\n### Handle different error types\\\\n```typescript\\\\nEffect.catchTags({\\\\n  ConfigError: (error) => Effect.fail(new MyServiceError('Invalid config', error)),\\\\n  NetworkError: (error) => Effect.fail(new MyServiceError('Network failed', error)),\\\\n  // Catch-all for unexpected errors\\\\n  UnknownException: (error) => Effect.fail(new MyServiceError('Unknown error', error))\\\\n})\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### Convert Promise errors safely\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// ✅ DO: Wrap promises properly\\\\nEffect.tryPromise({\\\\n  try: () => fetch(url),\\\\n  catch: (error) => new MyServiceError('Fetch failed', error)\\\\n})\\\\n\\\\n// ❌ DON'T: Use Effect.promise directly\\\\nEffect.promise(() => fetch(url)) // Loses error context\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n## Configuration Management\\\\n\\\\n### ✅ DO: Forbid fallbacks for sensitive data (Fix #022)\\\\n```typescript\\\\nexport const ApiKeyConfig = Config.string('API_KEY').pipe(\\\\n  Config.validate({\\\\n    message: 'API_KEY is required',\\\\n    validation: (key) => key.length > 0\\\\n  })\\\\n)\\\\n\\\\n// ❌ DON'T: Use withDefault for secrets\\\\nConfig.string('API_KEY').pipe(Config.withDefault('test-key'))\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### ✅ DO: Create typed configuration services\\\\n```typescript\\\\nexport interface MyServiceConfig {\\\\n  readonly apiUrl: string\\\\n  readonly timeout: number\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\nexport const MyServiceConfig = Context.GenericTag<MyServiceConfig>('MyServiceConfig')\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n## Testing Patterns\\\\n\\\\n### Use test layers properly\\\\n```typescript\\\\ndescribe('MyService', () => {\\\\n  const testLayer = Layer.mergeAll(\\\\n    DatabaseServiceTest,\\\\n    ConfigServiceTest,\\\\n    MyServiceLive\\\\n  )\\\\n  \\\\n  it('should work', async () => {\\\\n    const result = await Effect.runPromise(\\\\n      myServiceMethod.pipe(Effect.provide(testLayer))\\\\n    )\\\\n    expect(result).toBe(expected)\\\\n  })\\\\n})\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### Mock with spy capabilities\\\\n```typescript\\\\nconst mockLayer = createSpyMock(MyService, {\\\\n  doSomething: () => Effect.succeed('mocked')\\\\n})\\\\n\\\\n// After test\\\\nexpect(mockLayer.spy.calls.doSomething).toHaveLength(1)\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n## React Integration\\\\n\\\\n### ✅ DO: Use refs to prevent stale closures (Fix #023)\\\\n```typescript\\\\nconst runtimeRef = useRef(runtime)\\\\nuseEffect(() => {\\\\n  runtimeRef.current = runtime\\\\n}, [runtime])\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### ✅ DO: Cleanup Effect fibers\\\\n```typescript\\\\nuseEffect(() => {\\\\n  const fiber = Effect.runFork(myEffect)(runtime)\\\\n  return () => {\\\\n    fiber.interrupt\\\\n  }\\\\n}, [deps])\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### ❌ DON'T: Create runtime in render\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// Bad - creates new runtime every render\\\\nconst runtime = Runtime.make(layer)\\\\n\\\\n// Good - use useEffectRuntime hook\\\\nconst runtime = useEffectRuntime(layer)\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n## Common Gotchas\\\\n\\\\n### 1. Double-yield in Layer.effect (Fix #014)\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// ❌ Wrong - double yield\\\\nLayer.effect(\\\\n  MyService,\\\\n  Effect.gen(function* () {\\\\n    return yield* Effect.succeed(new MyServiceImpl())\\\\n  })\\\\n)\\\\n\\\\n// ✅ Correct - single yield or direct return\\\\nLayer.effect(\\\\n  MyService,\\\\n  Effect.gen(function* () {\\\\n    const config = yield* ConfigService\\\\n    return new MyServiceImpl(config)\\\\n  })\\\\n)\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### 2. Service dependency cycles\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// ❌ Circular dependency\\\\nconst ServiceA = Layer.effect(ServiceATag, \\\\n  Effect.gen(function* () {\\\\n    const b = yield* ServiceB // ServiceB depends on ServiceA!\\\\n  })\\\\n)\\\\n\\\\n// ✅ Use factory pattern or restructure\\\\nconst ServiceAFactory = (serviceB: ServiceB) => \\\\n  Layer.succeed(ServiceATag, new ServiceAImpl(serviceB))\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### 3. Effect vs Promise in APIs\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// When exposing to non-Effect code\\\\nexport class MyServiceBridge {\\\\n  async doSomething(input: string): Promise<Result> {\\\\n    return Effect.runPromise(\\\\n      this.service.doSomething(input).pipe(\\\\n        Effect.provide(this.runtime)\\\\n      )\\\\n    )\\\\n  }\\\\n}\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### 4. Type inference with pipe\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// Help TypeScript with explicit types when needed\\\\npipe(\\\\n  myEffect,\\\\n  Effect.map((value): ProcessedType => processValue(value)),\\\\n  Effect.catchAll((error): Effect.Effect<Fallback, never> => \\\\n    Effect.succeed(fallbackValue)\\\\n  )\\\\n)\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### 5. Testing async Effects\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// Use Effect.runPromise in tests\\\\nit('should handle async operations', async () => {\\\\n  const result = await Effect.runPromise(\\\\n    myAsyncEffect.pipe(Effect.provide(testLayer))\\\\n  )\\\\n  expect(result).toBe(expected)\\\\n})\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n## Quick Reference\\\\n\\\\n### Essential imports\\\\n```typescript\\\\nimport { Context, Effect, Layer, pipe, Stream } from 'effect'\\\\nimport * as Config from 'effect/Config'\\\\nimport * as Runtime from 'effect/Runtime'\\\\nimport * as Exit from 'effect/Exit'\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### Service lifecycle\\\\n1. Define interface → 2. Create Tag → 3. Implement → 4. Create Layer → 5. Compose\\\\n\\\\n### Effect operators by use case\\\\n- **Transform**: `map`, `flatMap`, `tap`\\\\n- **Error handling**: `catchAll`, `catchTags`, `orElse`\\\\n- **Resource management**: `acquireRelease`, `scoped`\\\\n- **Concurrency**: `all`, `race`, `forEach`\\\\n- **Retry**: `retry`, `retryOrElse`\\\\n- **Timeout**: `timeout`, `timeoutFail`\\\\n\\\\n### Debug helpers\\\\n```typescript\\\\n// Log intermediate values\\\\nEffect.tap(value => Effect.log('Value:', value))\\\\n\\\\n// Log and pass through\\\\nEffect.tapBoth({\\\\n  onFailure: (error) => Effect.log('Error:', error),\\\\n  onSuccess: (value) => Effect.log('Success:', value)\\\\n})\\\\n```\\\"}\"}},{\"id\":\"toolu_012Ay6RxguK9XhPHR4Uevfk6\",\"type\":\"function\",\"function\":{\"name\":\"Write\",\"arguments\":\"{\\\"file_path\\\":\\\"/Users/christopherdavid/code/commander/src/services/patterns/index.ts\\\",\\\"content\\\":\\\"/**\\\\n * Effect Service Pattern Library\\\\n * \\\\n * A collection of patterns and utilities for working with Effect-TS services\\\\n * in the OpenAgents Commander codebase.\\\\n */\\\\n\\\\n// Service creation patterns\\\\nexport * from './ServiceTemplate'\\\\n\\\\n// React integration hooks\\\\nexport * from './EffectReactHooks'\\\\n\\\\n// Testing utilities\\\\nexport * from './TestServiceMocks'\\\\n\\\\n// Re-export commonly used Effect types for convenience\\\\nexport { Context, Effect, Layer, pipe, Runtime } from 'effect'\\\\nexport type { ConfigError } from 'effect/ConfigError'\\\\nexport type { UnknownException } from 'effect/Cause'\\\"}\"}}]","timestamp":1748307521}}
