"content": "/\n * Effect React Hooks\n * \n * Safe patterns for using Effect services in React components\n * Addresses issues from docs/fixes/023-effect-runtime-stale-references.md\n */\n\nimport { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'\nimport { Effect, Exit, Runtime, pipe } from 'effect'\nimport type { Context } from 'effect'\n\n/\n * Hook for using Effect services in React components\n * Prevents stale reference issues by properly managing the runtime lifecycle\n */\nexport function useEffectService<A, E, R>(\n  effect: Effect.Effect<A, E, R>,\n  runtime: Runtime.Runtime<R>,\n  deps: React.DependencyList = []\n): {\n  data: A | null\n  error: E | null\n  loading: boolean\n  execute: () => void\n} {\n  const [data, setData] = useState<A | null>(null)\n  const [error, setError] = useState<E | null>(null)\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)\n  \n  // Use ref to store the latest runtime to prevent stale closures\n  const runtimeRef = useRef(runtime)\n  useEffect(() => {\n    runtimeRef.current = runtime\n  }, [runtime])\n  \n  // Store abort controller to cancel in-flight requests\n  const abortControllerRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null)\n  \n  const execute = useCallback(() => {\n    // Cancel any in-flight request\n    if (abortControllerRef.current) {\n      abortControllerRef.current.abort()\n    }\n    \n    const abortController = new AbortController()\n    abortControllerRef.current = abortController\n    \n    setLoading(true)\n    setError(null)\n    \n    const fiber = Effect.runFork(effect, {\n      signal: abortController.signal\n    })(runtimeRef.current)\n    \n    fiber.await.then((exit) => {\n      if (!abortController.signal.aborted) {\n        if (Exit.isSuccess(exit)) {\n          setData(exit.value)\n          setError(null)\n        } else {\n          setData(null)\n          setError(exit.cause as unknown as E)\n        }\n        setLoading(false)\n      }\n    })\n    \n    return () => {\n      abortController.abort()\n    }\n  }, [...deps, effect])\n  \n  // Cleanup on unmount\n  useEffect(() => {\n    return () => {\n      if (abortControllerRef.current) {\n        abortControllerRef.current.abort()\n      }\n    }\n  }, [])\n  \n  return { data, error, loading, execute }\n}\n\n/\n * Hook for using Effect services with automatic execution\n */\nexport function useEffectServiceAuto<A, E, R>(\n  effect: Effect.Effect<A, E, R>,\n  runtime: Runtime.Runtime<R>,\n  deps: React.DependencyList = []\n) {\n  const result = useEffectService(effect, runtime, deps)\n  \n  useEffect(() => {\n    result.execute()\n  }, [result.execute])\n  \n  return result\n}\n\n/\n * Hook for creating a stable Effect runtime in React\n * Prevents recreation on every render\n */\nexport function useEffectRuntime<R>(\n  layer: Effect.Layer<R, never, never>,\n  deps: React.DependencyList = []\n): Runtime.Runtime<R> | null {\n  const [runtime, setRuntime] = useState<Runtime.Runtime<R> | null>(null)\n  \n  useEffect(() => {\n    let canceled = false\n    \n    Effect.runPromise(\n      pipe(\n        Effect.runtime<R>(),\n        Effect.provide(layer)\n      )\n    ).then((rt) => {\n      if (!canceled) {\n        setRuntime(rt)\n      }\n    })\n    \n    return () => {\n      canceled = true\n    }\n  }, deps)\n  \n  return runtime\n}\n\n/\n * Hook for accessing a specific service from the runtime\n */\nexport function useService<T>(\n  tag: Context.Tag<T, T>,\n  runtime: Runtime.Runtime<any> | null\n): T | null {\n  const [service, setService] = useState<T | null>(null)\n  \n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (runtime) {\n      const context = runtime.context\n      if (Context.isContext(context)) {\n        const serviceInstance = Context.getOption(context, tag)\n        if (serviceInstance._tag === 'Some') {\n          setService(serviceInstance.value)\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }, [runtime, tag])\n  \n  return service\n}\n\n/\n * Hook for streaming Effect values\n * Useful for real-time updates, progress tracking, etc.\n */\nexport function useEffectStream<A, E, R>(\n  stream: Effect.Stream<A, E, R>,\n  runtime: Runtime.Runtime<R>,\n  onValue: (value: A) => void,\n  onError?: (error: E) => void,\n  deps: React.DependencyList = []\n) {\n  const runtimeRef = useRef(runtime)\n  useEffect(() => {\n    runtimeRef.current = runtime\n  }, [runtime])\n  \n  useEffect(() => {\n    const fiber = Effect.runFork(\n      stream.pipe(\n        Effect.tap((value) => Effect.sync(() => onValue(value))),\n        Effect.catchAll((error) =>\n          Effect.sync(() => {\n            if (onError) onError(error)\n          })\n        )\n      )\n    )(runtimeRef.current)\n    \n    return () => {\n      fiber.interrupt\n    }\n  }, deps)\n}\n\n/\n * Error boundary component for Effect services\n */\nexport class EffectErrorBoundary extends React.Component<\n  {\n    children: React.ReactNode\n    fallback: (error: unknown) => React.ReactNode\n  },\n  { hasError: boolean; error: unknown }\n> {\n  constructor(props: any) {\n    super(props)\n    this.state = { hasError: false, error: null }\n  }\n  \n  static getDerivedStateFromError(error: unknown) {\n    return { hasError: true, error }\n  }\n  \n  componentDidCatch(error: Error, errorInfo: React.ErrorInfo) {\n    console.error('Effect service error:', error, errorInfo)\n  }\n  \n  render() {\n    if (this.state.hasError) {\n      return this.props.fallback(this.state.error)\n    }\n    \n    return this.props.children\n  }\n}"
}

"content": "/\n * Test Service Mocks Template\n * \n * Patterns for creating test mocks that satisfy Effect's type system\n * Addresses issues from docs/fixes/012-strategic-test-type-casting.md\n */\n\nimport { Context, Effect, Layer, pipe } from 'effect'\nimport type { ConfigError } from 'effect/ConfigError'\n\n/\n * Pattern 1: Simple mock with static values\n */\nexport const createSimpleMock = <T>(\n  tag: Context.Tag<T, T>,\n  implementation: T\n): Layer.Layer<T> => {\n  return Layer.succeed(tag, implementation)\n}\n\n/\n * Pattern 2: Mock with configurable behavior\n */\nexport interface MockConfig<T> {\n  implementation: Partial<T>\n  delays?: Record<string, number>\n  errors?: Record<string, Error>\n}\n\nexport const createConfigurableMock = <T extends Record<string, any>>(\n  tag: Context.Tag<T, T>,\n  config: MockConfig<T>\n): Layer.Layer<T> => {\n  const implementation = {} as T\n  \n  for (const key in config.implementation) {\n    const method = config.implementation[key]\n    if (typeof method === 'function') {\n      implementation[key] = ((...args: any[]) => {\n        // Add configured delay\n        const delay = config.delays?.[key] || 0\n        \n        // Check for configured error\n        const error = config.errors?.[key]\n        \n        return pipe(\n          Effect.sleep(delay),\n          Effect.flatMap(() => \n            error \n              ? Effect.fail(error)\n              : Effect.succeed(method(...args))\n          )\n        )\n      }) as any\n    } else {\n      implementation[key] = method as any\n    }\n  }\n  \n  return Layer.succeed(tag, implementation)\n}\n\n/\n * Pattern 3: Spy mock that records calls\n */\nexport interface SpyMock<T> {\n  implementation: T\n  calls: Record<string, any[][]>\n  resetCalls: () => void\n}\n\nexport const createSpyMock = <T extends Record<string, any>>(\n  tag: Context.Tag<T, T>,\n  baseImplementation: T\n): Layer.Layer<T> & { spy: SpyMock<T> } => {\n  const calls: Record<string, any[][]> = {}\n  \n  const implementation = {} as T\n  \n  for (const key in baseImplementation) {\n    const method = baseImplementation[key]\n    if (typeof method === 'function') {\n      calls[key] = []\n      implementation[key] = ((...args: any[]) => {\n        calls[key].push(args)\n        return method(...args)\n      }) as any\n    } else {\n      implementation[key] = method\n    }\n  }\n  \n  const spy: SpyMock<T> = {\n    implementation,\n    calls,\n    resetCalls: () => {\n      for (const key in calls) {\n        calls[key] = []\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  \n  const layer = Layer.succeed(tag, implementation) as Layer.Layer<T> & { spy: SpyMock<T> }\n  layer.spy = spy\n  \n  return layer\n}\n\n/\n * Pattern 4: State-based mock\n */\nexport interface StatefulMock<T, S> {\n  implementation: T\n  getState: () => S\n  setState: (state: S) => void\n  resetState: () => void\n}\n\nexport const createStatefulMock = <T extends Record<string, any>, S>(\n  tag: Context.Tag<T, T>,\n  initialState: S,\n  implementationFactory: (\n    getState: () => S,\n    setState: (state: S) => void\n  ) => T\n): Layer.Layer<T> & { mock: StatefulMock<T, S> } => {\n  let state = initialState\n  \n  const getState = () => state\n  const setState = (newState: S) => { state = newState }\n  const resetState = () => { state = initialState }\n  \n  const implementation = implementationFactory(getState, setState)\n  \n  const mock: StatefulMock<T, S> = {\n    implementation,\n    getState,\n    setState,\n    resetState\n  }\n  \n  const layer = Layer.succeed(tag, implementation) as Layer.Layer<T> & { mock: StatefulMock<T, S> }\n  layer.mock = mock\n  \n  return layer\n}\n\n/\n * Pattern 5: Async mock with promise resolution control\n /\nexport interface AsyncMock<T> {\n  implementation: T\n  resolvers: Record<string, {\n    resolve: (value: any) => void\n    reject: (error: any) => void\n  }[]>\n  waitForCall: (method: string, callIndex?: number) => Promise<void>\n}\n\nexport const createAsyncMock = <T extends Record<string, any>>(\n  tag: Context.Tag<T, T>,\n  methods: (keyof T)[]\n): Layer.Layer<T> & { async: AsyncMock<T> } => {\n  const resolvers: Record<string, any[]> = {}\n  const pendingCalls: Record<string, Promise<void>[]> = {}\n  \n  const implementation = {} as T\n  \n  for (const method of methods) {\n    resolvers[method as string] = []\n    pendingCalls[method as string] = []\n    \n    implementation[method as string] = (() => {\n      return Effect.async((resume) => {\n        const promise = new Promise<void>((resolve) => {\n          const resolverObj = {\n            resolve: (value: any) => {\n              resume(Effect.succeed(value))\n              resolve()\n            },\n            reject: (error: any) => {\n              resume(Effect.fail(error))\n              resolve()\n            }\n          }\n          resolvers[method as string].push(resolverObj)\n        })\n        pendingCalls[method as string].push(promise)\n      })\n    }) as any\n  }\n  \n  const asyncMock: AsyncMock<T> = {\n    implementation,\n    resolvers: resolvers as any,\n    waitForCall: async (method: string, callIndex = 0) => {\n      const calls = pendingCalls[method]\n      if (calls && calls[callIndex]) {\n        await calls[callIndex]\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  \n  const layer = Layer.succeed(tag, implementation) as Layer.Layer<T> & { async: AsyncMock<T> }\n  layer.async = asyncMock\n  \n  return layer\n}\n\n/**\n * Example usage with a hypothetical UserService\n /\nexport interface UserService {\n  getUser: (id: string) => Effect.Effect<{ id: string; name: string }, Error>\n  updateUser: (id: string, name: string) => Effect.Effect<void, Error>\n}\n\nexport const UserService = Context.GenericTag<UserService>('UserService')\n\n// Example 1: Simple mock\nexport const UserServiceSimpleMock = createSimpleMock(UserService, {\n  getUser: (id: string) => Effect.succeed({ id, name: 'Test User' }),\n  updateUser: () => Effect.succeed(undefined)\n})\n\n// Example 2: Spy mock\nexport const UserServiceSpyMock = createSpyMock(UserService, {\n  getUser: (id: string) => Effect.succeed({ id, name: 'Test User' }),\n  updateUser: () => Effect.succeed(undefined)\n})\n\n// Example 3: Stateful mock\nexport const UserServiceStatefulMock = createStatefulMock(\n  UserService,\n  { users: new Map<string, string>() },\n  (getState, setState) => ({\n    getUser: (id: string) =>\n      Effect.gen(function () {\n        const state = getState()\n        const name = state.users.get(id)\n        if (!name) {\n          return yield Effect.fail(new Error('User not found'))\n        }\n        return { id, name }\n      }),\n    updateUser: (id: string, name: string) =>\n      Effect.sync(() => {\n        const state = getState()\n        state.users.set(id, name)\n        setState(state)\n      })\n  })\n)\n\n// Test helpers\nexport const runEffectTest = async <A, E>(\n  effect: Effect.Effect<A, E, any>,\n  layer: Layer.Layer<any>\n): Promise<A> => {\n  return Effect.runPromise(pipe(effect, Effect.provide(layer)))\n}\n\nexport const runEffectTestExpectError = async <A, E>(\n  effect: Effect.Effect<A, E, any>,\n  layer: Layer.Layer<any>\n): Promise<E> => {\n  try {\n    await Effect.runPromise(pipe(effect, Effect.provide(layer)))\n    throw new Error('Expected effect to fail')\n  } catch (error) {\n    return error as E\n  }\n}"
}

"file_path": "/Users/christopherdavid/code/commander/src/services/patterns/EffectCheatSheet.md",
"content": "# Effect-TS Cheat Sheet for OpenAgents Commander\n\nThis cheat sheet covers common Effect patterns and solutions to issues found in docs/fixes/.\n\n## Table of Contents\n1. Service Creation Patterns\n2. Error Handling  \n3. Configuration Management\n4. Testing Patterns\n5. React Integration\n6. Common Gotchas\n\n## Service Creation Patterns\n\n### ✅ DO: Use Context.GenericTag\ntypescript\n// Correct - provides better type inference\nexport const MyService = Context.GenericTag<MyService>('MyService')\n\n\n### ❌ DON'T: Use Context.Tag\ntypescript\n// Incorrect - can cause type inference issues\nexport const MyService = Context.Tag<MyService>('MyService')\n\n\n### ✅ DO: Create specific error types\ntypescript\nexport class MyServiceError extends Error {\n  readonly _tag = 'MyServiceError'\n  constructor(message: string, readonly cause?: unknown) {\n    super(message)\n  }\n}\n\n\n### ✅ DO: Use Layer.effect for complex services\ntypescript\nexport const MyServiceLive = Layer.effect(\n  MyService,\n  Effect.gen(function* () {\n    const config = yield* ConfigService\n    const database = yield* DatabaseService\n    return new MyServiceImpl(config, database)\n  })\n)\n\n\n## Error Handling\n\n### Handle different error types\ntypescript\nEffect.catchTags({\n  ConfigError: (error) => Effect.fail(new MyServiceError('Invalid config', error)),\n  NetworkError: (error) => Effect.fail(new MyServiceError('Network failed', error)),\n  // Catch-all for unexpected errors\n  UnknownException: (error) => Effect.fail(new MyServiceError('Unknown error', error))\n})\n\n\n### Convert Promise errors safely\ntypescript\n// ✅ DO: Wrap promises properly\nEffect.tryPromise({\n  try: () => fetch(url),\n  catch: (error) => new MyServiceError('Fetch failed', error)\n})\n\n// ❌ DON'T: Use Effect.promise directly\nEffect.promise(() => fetch(url)) // Loses error context\n\n\n## Configuration Management\n\n### ✅ DO: Forbid fallbacks for sensitive data (Fix #022)\ntypescript\nexport const ApiKeyConfig = Config.string('API_KEY').pipe(\n  Config.validate({\n    message: 'API_KEY is required',\n    validation: (key) => key.length > 0\n  })\n)\n\n// ❌ DON'T: Use withDefault for secrets\nConfig.string('API_KEY').pipe(Config.withDefault('test-key'))\n\n\n### ✅ DO: Create typed configuration services\ntypescript\nexport interface MyServiceConfig {\n  readonly apiUrl: string\n  readonly timeout: number\n}\n\nexport const MyServiceConfig = Context.GenericTag<MyServiceConfig>('MyServiceConfig')\n\n\n## Testing Patterns\n\n### Use test layers properly\ntypescript\ndescribe('MyService', () => {\n  const testLayer = Layer.mergeAll(\n    DatabaseServiceTest,\n    ConfigServiceTest,\n    MyServiceLive\n  )\n  \n  it('should work', async () => {\n    const result = await Effect.runPromise(\n      myServiceMethod.pipe(Effect.provide(testLayer))\n    )\n    expect(result).toBe(expected)\n  })\n})\n\n\n### Mock with spy capabilities\ntypescript\nconst mockLayer = createSpyMock(MyService, {\n  doSomething: () => Effect.succeed('mocked')\n})\n\n// After test\nexpect(mockLayer.spy.calls.doSomething).toHaveLength(1)\n\n\n## React Integration\n\n### ✅ DO: Use refs to prevent stale closures (Fix #023)\ntypescript\nconst runtimeRef = useRef(runtime)\nuseEffect(() => {\n  runtimeRef.current = runtime\n}, [runtime])\n\n\n### ✅ DO: Cleanup Effect fibers\ntypescript\nuseEffect(() => {\n  const fiber = Effect.runFork(myEffect)(runtime)\n  return () => {\n    fiber.interrupt\n  }\n}, [deps])\n\n\n### ❌ DON'T: Create runtime in render\ntypescript\n// Bad - creates new runtime every render\nconst runtime = Runtime.make(layer)\n\n// Good - use useEffectRuntime hook\nconst runtime = useEffectRuntime(layer)\n\n\n## Common Gotchas\n\n### 1. Double-yield in Layer.effect (Fix #014)\ntypescript\n// ❌ Wrong - double yield\nLayer.effect(\n  MyService,\n  Effect.gen(function* () {\n    return yield* Effect.succeed(new MyServiceImpl())\n  })\n)\n\n// ✅ Correct - single yield or direct return\nLayer.effect(\n  MyService,\n  Effect.gen(function* () {\n    const config = yield* ConfigService\n    return new MyServiceImpl(config)\n  })\n)\n\n\n### 2. Service dependency cycles\ntypescript\n// ❌ Circular dependency\nconst ServiceA = Layer.effect(ServiceATag, \n  Effect.gen(function* () {\n    const b = yield* ServiceB // ServiceB depends on ServiceA!\n  })\n)\n\n// ✅ Use factory pattern or restructure\nconst ServiceAFactory = (serviceB: ServiceB) => \n  Layer.succeed(ServiceATag, new ServiceAImpl(serviceB))\n\n\n### 3. Effect vs Promise in APIs\ntypescript\n// When exposing to non-Effect code\nexport class MyServiceBridge {\n  async doSomething(input: string): Promise<Result> {\n    return Effect.runPromise(\n      this.service.doSomething(input).pipe(\n        Effect.provide(this.runtime)\n      )\n    )\n  }\n}\n\n\n### 4. Type inference with pipe\ntypescript\n// Help TypeScript with explicit types when needed\npipe(\n  myEffect,\n  Effect.map((value): ProcessedType => processValue(value)),\n  Effect.catchAll((error): Effect.Effect<Fallback, never> => \n    Effect.succeed(fallbackValue)\n  )\n)\n\n\n### 5. Testing async Effects\ntypescript\n// Use Effect.runPromise in tests\nit('should handle async operations', async () => {\n  const result = await Effect.runPromise(\n    myAsyncEffect.pipe(Effect.provide(testLayer))\n  )\n  expect(result).toBe(expected)\n})\n\n\n## Quick Reference\n\n### Essential imports\ntypescript\nimport { Context, Effect, Layer, pipe, Stream } from 'effect'\nimport * as Config from 'effect/Config'\nimport * as Runtime from 'effect/Runtime'\nimport * as Exit from 'effect/Exit'\n\n\n### Service lifecycle\n1. Define interface → 2. Create Tag → 3. Implement → 4. Create Layer → 5. Compose\n\n### Effect operators by use case\n- Transform: map, flatMap, tap\n- Error handling: catchAll, catchTags, orElse\n- Resource management: acquireRelease, scoped\n- Concurrency: all, race, forEach\n- Retry: retry, retryOrElse\n- Timeout: timeout, timeoutFail\n\n### Debug helpers\ntypescript\n// Log intermediate values\nEffect.tap(value => Effect.log('Value:', value))\n\n// Log and pass through\nEffect.tapBoth({\n  onFailure: (error) => Effect.log('Error:', error),\n  onSuccess: (value) => Effect.log('Success:', value)\n})\n"
}
